#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}

+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+

+struct Panel {
+    size_t start, width;
+    int aRoot, bRoot;
+};

+struct PanelBuffer {
+    std::vector<double> a, b;
+    MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+};
+
+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);

+void multiplyTile(const double* a, const double* b, double* c,
+                  size_t stride, size_t width) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d c00 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c);
+    __m256d c01 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 4);
+    __m256d c10 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride);
+    __m256d c11 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride + 4);
+    __m256d c20 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride);
+    __m256d c21 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4);
+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);
+        multiplyPanel(current, C, grid, panels[p].width,
+                      hasNext ? next.requests : nullptr);
+    }
+}
+

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }
+}

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0;

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}
+
+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.cols; ++j) {
+            mat[i * grid.cols + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, grid.rowStart + i,
+                                                       grid.colStart + j);
         }
     }

+struct Panel {
+    size_t start, width;
+    int aRoot, bRoot;
+};
+
+struct PanelBuffer {
+    std::vector<double> a, b;
+    MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+};

+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);
 }

+void multiplyTile(const double* a, const double* b, double* c,
+                  size_t stride, size_t width) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d c00 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c);
+    __m256d c01 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 4);
+    __m256d c10 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride);
+    __m256d c11 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride + 4);
+    __m256d c20 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride);
+    __m256d c21 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4);
+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);
+        multiplyPanel(current, C, grid, panels[p].width,
+                      hasNext ? next.requests : nullptr);
+    }
+}
+

             }

-            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
+            const double actual = C[(i - grid.rowStart) * grid.paddedCols + j - grid.colStart];
             const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-

+            if (!std::isfinite(actual) || relError > 1e-6) {

             }

 }
 
+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }
+}

codex
The MPI implementation is in place: ranks hold local blocks, broadcast packed panels, and overlap the next transfers with local multiplication. Validation runs on the ranks that own the checked elements; a full result is gathered only for `-r`. I’ll now check builds, uneven dimensions, and runs with more ranks than matrix rows.

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}
+
+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.cols; ++j) {
+            mat[i * grid.cols + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, grid.rowStart + i,
+                                                       grid.colStart + j);
         }
     }

+struct Panel {
+    size_t start, width;
+    int aRoot, bRoot;
+};
+
+struct PanelBuffer {
+    std::vector<double> a, b;
+    MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+};
+
+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);
 }

-bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

-    // Check a few random positions
-    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
-    
+void multiplyTile(const double* a, const double* b, double* c,
+                  size_t stride, size_t width) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d c00 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c);
+    __m256d c01 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 4);
+    __m256d c10 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride);
+    __m256d c11 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride + 4);
+    __m256d c20 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride);
+    __m256d c21 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4);
+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}
+
+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        // A rank that throws must reach MPI_Abort instead of entering collective frees.
+        if (std::uncaught_exceptions()) return;
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {

+            mat[i * grid.cols + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, grid.rowStart + i,

+struct Panel {
+    size_t start, width;
+    int aRoot, bRoot;
+};

+struct PanelBuffer {
+    std::vector<double> a, b;
+    MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+};

+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);
 }

+void multiplyTile(const double* a, const double* b, double* c,
+                  size_t stride, size_t width) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d c00 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c);
+    __m256d c01 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 4);
+    __m256d c10 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride);
+    __m256d c11 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + stride + 4);
+    __m256d c20 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride);
+    __m256d c21 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4);
+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);
+        multiplyPanel(current, C, grid, panels[p].width,
+                      hasNext ? next.requests : nullptr);
+    }
+}
+

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }
+}

import concurrent.futures, math, re, subprocess, sys
root = sys.argv[1]
exe = root + '/build/matmul'
cases = {1: [1,2,4,9], 2: [1,3,8], 3: [1,4,6,16], 7: [1,2,7], 17: [1,3,4,6,8], 64: [1,4], 65: [1,3,4], 257: [1,2,4,6], 513: [1,3,4,6]}

def stats(output):
    match = re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===\\n(.*?)=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    assert match, output
    fields = dict(line.split(': ', 1) for line in match.group(1).splitlines())
    return fields
refs = {n: stats(subprocess.check_output([root + '/matmul-original', '-n', str(n), '-r', '-v'], text=True)) for n in cases}

def check(item):
    n, ranks = item
    cmd = ['mpiexec', '--oversubscribe', '-n', str(ranks), exe, '-n', str(n), '-r', '-v']
    run = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
    assert run.returncode == 0, (cmd, run.stdout, run.stderr)
    assert run.stdout.count('Matrix Multiplication Benchmark') == 1, run.stdout
    assert run.stdout.count('Validation: PASSED') == 1, run.stdout
    values = stats(run.stdout)
    for key, expected in refs[n].items():
        if key == 'Hash':
            continue
        if key in ('Name', 'Elements'):
            assert values[key] == expected, (n, ranks, key, values[key], expected)
        else:
            assert math.isclose(float(values[key]), float(expected), rel_tol=1e-12, abs_tol=1e-12), (n, ranks, key, values[key], expected)
    return n, ranks, values['Hash']

items = [(n,p) for n, ps in cases.items() for p in ps]
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
    results = list(pool.map(check, items))
for n in cases:
    hashes = {h for size, p, h in results if size == n}
    assert len(hashes) == 1, (n, hashes)
print(f'PASS: {len(results)} MPI cases match original statistics and samples; result hashes are identical across rank counts.')

+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}
+
+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        // A rank that throws must reach MPI_Abort instead of entering collective frees.
+        if (std::uncaught_exceptions()) return;
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};

+    size_t start, width;

+struct PanelBuffer {
+    std::vector<double> a, b;
+    MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+};

+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }

+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);

+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);
+        multiplyPanel(current, C, grid, panels[p].width,
+                      hasNext ? next.requests : nullptr);
+    }
+}

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }
+}

+constexpr size_t tileRows = 4;
+constexpr size_t tileCols = 8;
+constexpr size_t panelSize = 256;
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate + std::min(N % parts, size_t(coordinate));
+}
+
+size_t paddedSize(size_t n, size_t tile) {
+    return ((n + tile - 1) / tile) * tile;
+}
+
+struct Grid {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2] = {0, 0};
+    size_t rowStart = 0, colStart = 0, rows = 0, cols = 0;
+    size_t paddedRows = 0, paddedCols = 0;
+
+    explicit Grid(size_t N) {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rowStart = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        colStart = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1]);
+        rows = blockStart(N, dims[0], coords[0] + 1) - rowStart;
+        cols = blockStart(N, dims[1], coords[1] + 1) - colStart;
+        paddedRows = paddedSize(rows, tileRows);
+        paddedCols = paddedSize(cols, tileCols);
+    }
+
+    ~Grid() {
+        // A rank that throws must reach MPI_Abort instead of entering collective frees.
+        if (std::uncaught_exceptions()) return;
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};

+std::vector<Panel> makePanels(size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Every panel lies within both an A column block and a B row block.
+    // Bounding the padded message sizes also keeps MPI counts within INT_MAX.
+    const size_t largestBlock = std::max(
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], 1), tileRows),
+        paddedSize(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], 1), tileCols));
+    const size_t width = std::min(panelSize, size_t(INT_MAX) / largestBlock);
+    std::vector<Panel> panels;
+    int aRoot = 0, bRoot = 0;
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N;) {
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1) <= k) ++aRoot;
+        while (blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1) <= k) ++bRoot;
+        const size_t end = std::min({N, k + width,
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], aRoot + 1),
+            blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], bRoot + 1)});
+        panels.push_back({k, end - k, aRoot, bRoot});
+        k = end;
+    }
+    return panels;
+}
+
+void broadcastPanel(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    const Grid& grid, const Panel& panel, PanelBuffer& buffer) {
+    // Broadcast in microkernel layout, avoiding a second packing pass at receivers.
+    if (grid.coords[1] == panel.aRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.colStart;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.paddedRows; i += tileRows) {
+            double* packed = buffer.a.data() + i * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r) {
+                    packed[k * tileRows + r] = i + r < grid.rows
+                        ? A[(i + r) * grid.cols + offset + k] : 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if (grid.coords[0] == panel.bRoot) {
+        const size_t offset = panel.start - grid.rowStart;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < grid.paddedCols; j += tileCols) {
+            double* packed = buffer.b.data() + j * panel.width;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < panel.width; ++k) {
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < tileCols; ++c) {
+                    packed[k * tileCols + c] = j + c < grid.cols
+                        ? B[(offset + k) * grid.cols + j + c] : 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.a.data(), int(grid.paddedRows * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.aRoot, grid.rowComm, &buffer.requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(buffer.b.data(), int(grid.paddedCols * panel.width), MPI_DOUBLE,
+               panel.bRoot, grid.colComm, &buffer.requests[1]);

+    __m256d c20 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride);
+    __m256d c21 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4);
+    __m256d c30 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride);
+    __m256d c31 = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols) {
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(b + 4);
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 1);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 2);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + 3);
+        c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+        c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+        c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+        c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+        c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+        c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+        c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+        c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+    }
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c, c00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 4, c01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride, c10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + stride + 4, c11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride, c20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 2 * stride + 4, c21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride, c30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + 3 * stride + 4, c31);
+#else
+    // Portable kernel: vectorize across columns without reassociating the k sum.
+    double sums[tileRows][tileCols];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(c + r * stride, tileCols, sums[r]);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k, a += tileRows, b += tileCols)
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < tileCols; ++j)
+                sums[r][j] += a[r] * b[j];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < tileRows; ++r)
+        std::copy_n(sums[r], tileCols, c + r * stride);
+#endif
+}
+
+void multiplyPanel(const PanelBuffer& buffer, std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const Grid& grid, size_t width, MPI_Request* pending) {
+    // Keep the working A/B panels and C tiles in the private cache.
+    constexpr size_t cacheRows = 64, cacheCols = 64;
+    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < grid.paddedCols; jj += cacheCols) {
+        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < grid.paddedRows; ii += cacheRows) {
+            const size_t endI = std::min(ii + cacheRows, grid.paddedRows);
+            const size_t endJ = std::min(jj + cacheCols, grid.paddedCols);
+            for (size_t j = jj; j < endJ; j += tileCols) {
+                for (size_t i = ii; i < endI; i += tileRows) {
+                    multiplyTile(buffer.a.data() + i * width,
+                                 buffer.b.data() + j * width,
+                                 C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols + j,
+                                 grid.paddedCols, width);
+                }
+            }
+            // MPI implementations without background progress still overlap transfers.
+            if (pending) {
+                int complete = 0;
+                MPI_Testall(2, pending, &complete, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+                if (complete) pending = nullptr;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                    std::vector<double>& C, const Grid& grid,
+                    const std::vector<Panel>& panels, std::array<PanelBuffer, 2>& buffers) {
+    // Pipelined SUMMA: receive the next panels while computing the current ones.
+    broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[0], buffers[0]);
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels.size(); ++p) {
+        PanelBuffer& current = buffers[p % 2];
+        PanelBuffer& next = buffers[(p + 1) % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const bool hasNext = p + 1 < panels.size();
+        if (hasNext) broadcastPanel(A, B, grid, panels[p + 1], next);
+        multiplyPanel(current, C, grid, panels[p].width,
+                      hasNext ? next.requests : nullptr);
+    }
+}

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, const Grid& grid) {
+    // Only -r materializes the full row-major result, preserving statistics and hash.
+    // Derived types avoid packing and the INT_MAX total-element limit of Gatherv.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows; ++i)
+            std::copy_n(C.data() + i * grid.paddedCols, grid.cols, result.data() + i * N);
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
+            int coords[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coords);
+            int sizes[2] = {int(N), int(N)};
+            int starts[2] = {int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0])),
+                             int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1]))};
+            int subsizes[2] = {
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[0], coords[0] + 1)) - starts[0],
+                int(blockStart(N, grid.dims[1], coords[1] + 1)) - starts[1]};
+            if (!subsizes[0] || !subsizes[1]) continue;
+            MPI_Datatype block;
+            MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, sizes, subsizes, starts, MPI_ORDER_C,
+                                     MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, block, rank, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&block);
+        }
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    } else if (grid.rows && grid.cols) {
+        MPI_Datatype block;
+        MPI_Type_vector(int(grid.rows), int(grid.cols), int(grid.paddedCols), MPI_DOUBLE, &block);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+        MPI_Send(C.data(), 1, block, 0, 0, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&block);
+    }
+}
